# Commands · M · part 6

1,415 entries, mockup to mr. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **mockup**: Design checkpoint — propose 2–3 mockup variants of a user-facing surface (web artboards, CLI storyboard, API examples, schema, architecture spine), render them, get the user's pick (headless: the recommended default), and freeze it as the design contract in .agentic/design.md. Called by /cas:build and /cas:prototype; usable standalone. ([grappeq/claude-agentic-stack](https://github.com/grappeq/claude-agentic-stack/blob/HEAD/commands/mockup.md))
- **mockup**: Tu vas générer des maquettes fonctionnelles (composants React) à partir des user stories ou du FEATURES.md. ([Ri087/combien-tu-nous-coutes.com](https://github.com/Ri087/combien-tu-nous-coutes.com/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/mockup.md) · 2 copies)
- **mockup**: Vas a construir un mockup de UI del producto Livio como componente JSX/Tailwind. Estos mockups son el asset visual más importante de la landing — deben verse como software real funcionando, no como wireframes ni screenshots. ([tomiloki/LivioWeb](https://github.com/tomiloki/LivioWeb/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/mockup.md))
- **mockup-from-plan**: This command should be used when the user asks to 'build a mockup from a plan', 'generate mockup from plan', 'create HTML mockup', or 'compare mockup against plan'. Runs one iteration: compares an HTML/CSS/JS mockup against a plan document, identifies gaps, and fixes them. ([neonwatty/useful-loops](https://github.com/neonwatty/useful-loops/blob/HEAD/commands/mockup-from-plan.md))
- **mockup-from-plan-loop**: This command should be used when the user asks to 'loop mockup from plan', 'keep building mockup until it matches the plan', or 'iteratively close mockup gaps'. Loops mockup-from-plan iterations until the mockup fully matches the plan. Requires ralph-loop plugin. ([neonwatty/useful-loops](https://github.com/neonwatty/useful-loops/blob/HEAD/commands/mockup-from-plan-loop.md))
- **mockup-gallery**: Single entry point for mockup-gallery. Use this command to launch review, inspect state, manage sessions, prepare implementation handoff, or decide which mockup-gallery capability to use. ([tyroneross/mockup-gallery](https://github.com/tyroneross/mockup-gallery/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/mockup-gallery.md) · 2 copies)
- **mockup-implementation**: Instructions for implementing mockups ([Msamir22/Monyvi](https://github.com/Msamir22/Monyvi/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/mockup-implementation.md))
- **mockup-init**: Scaffolds the mockup-review framework in the current project (creates .preview/mockups/ with server, JS, CSS assets). ([ziroparallelo/mockup-review](https://github.com/ziroparallelo/mockup-review/blob/HEAD/commands/mockup-init.md))
- **mockup-serve**: Starts the mockup-review HTTP server on port 8765 (bound to 127.0.0.1) from the current project. ([ziroparallelo/mockup-review](https://github.com/ziroparallelo/mockup-review/blob/HEAD/commands/mockup-serve.md))
- **mockup-stop**: Stops the mockup-review HTTP server (port 8765) and optionally cleans up .preview/ directory. ([ziroparallelo/mockup-review](https://github.com/ziroparallelo/mockup-review/blob/HEAD/commands/mockup-stop.md))
- **mockup-test**: Gate 1 — the mockup test. $12.50/day, Thu-Sun, 5 ads, winner is sub-$1 cost per link click. ([samnguyen80/pod-brain-we-scale](https://github.com/samnguyen80/pod-brain-we-scale/blob/HEAD/plugins/wescale-pod-knowledge/commands/mockup-test.md))
- **mockups**: Research, draft or review this feature's surfaces, storyboard them, and get each state approved before anything is modelled or charted ([ROBCOATVG/slipwai](https://github.com/ROBCOATVG/slipwai/blob/HEAD/assets/toolkit/commands/mockups.md) · ★5)

- **mod.rs**: Just module declarations: `auth`, `config`, `deep_link`, `file_download`, `health`, `netmind_oauth`, `notify`, `power`, `service`, `tray`, `updater`, `artifact_fetch`, `office_watch_scheme`. No logic. Each module exposes one or more `#[tauri::command]` functions registered in `lib.rs::run`'s `invoke_handler!` (or, for `office_watch_scheme`, a custom-scheme handler registered on the builder). `deep ([NetMindAI-Open/NarraNexus](https://github.com/NetMindAI-Open/NarraNexus/blob/HEAD/.mindflow/mirror/tauri/src-tauri/src/commands/mod.rs.md) · ★91)

- **Modal Subtraction**: Generate 20 candidate angles, strip everything that overlaps with the 5 most common takes, then stress-test what survives. Produces the angle nobody else is hitting. From Lou's Sakana LCM walkthrough, May 21 session. ([coachlou/ambient-library](https://github.com/coachlou/ambient-library/blob/HEAD/library/aimm-commands/commands/modal-subtraction.md))
- **modal-interchange**: re:Harmoniz cross-scope mashup — borrow knowledge across two research scopes and mint cross-domain insights, like borrowing chords from a parallel mode. A crossover serving no open decision becomes a question, not a mashup. ([hwang-yeonsu/re-harmoniz](https://github.com/hwang-yeonsu/re-harmoniz/blob/HEAD/commands/modal-interchange.md))
- **modal-subtraction**: Generate 20 candidate angles, strip everything that overlaps with the 5 most common takes, then stress-test what survives. Produces the angle nobody else is hitting. From Lou's Sakana LCM walkthrough, May 21 session. ([kosif199022-jpg/kosif-atlas](https://github.com/kosif199022-jpg/kosif-atlas/blob/HEAD/skills/1120-ambient-library/2593-aimm-commands/commands/modal-subtraction.md) · 2 copies)
- **modcod-pareto**: Build the Pareto frontier over modulation-and-coding choices and pick the cost-benefit-optimal operating point. Climbing the MODCOD table (QPSK → 8PSK → 16/32APSK, lower → higher code rate) buys spectral efficiency but demands more Es/N₀ — the satcom version of opening the aperture for more light while losing depth of field. Adaptive Coding and Modulation (ACM) lets the link **stop down dynamicall ([DaxxSec/cognitropy-lab](https://github.com/DaxxSec/cognitropy-lab/blob/HEAD/satellite-communication-protocols/.claude/commands/modcod-pareto.md) · ★1)

- **module-audit**: Pre-ship module audit pipeline. 7-stage matrix audit covering form components × 4 lenses + 10 cross-cutting axes + adversarial layer + SHA-anchored verification. Recon freezes one AUDIT_SHA; the Stage 4.5 verifier checks findings against that commit via git show (not HEAD) to catch snapshot incoherence. Use ONLY for module-completion sweeps with ≥3 form components. For single-page work use /page-design or /page-code; for changes meeting the project's Heavy-rigor trigger use /change-heavy; for every other change or FU cleanup use /cleanup-light. ([fire-farmer/red-line-toolkit](https://github.com/fire-farmer/red-line-toolkit/blob/HEAD/commands/module-audit.md))
